# 2. Holds and readiness conditions

## 2.1 Features on hold and readiness conditions

These features are in scope, and most are P1, but each is held by an open decision. A developer does not start them until the decision is recorded. The Product PRD Appendix A shows the decision a feature is on hold for (column "On hold").

**Hold rule .** An open decision holds a feature when it decides (a) whether the feature is in scope or in P1, (b) the build approach or the product the feature is built on, (c) the external counterpart without which the feature cannot be built (a bank, gateway, regulator position or hosting constraint), or (d) client content that the feature's criteria need. A hold propagates: a feature that depends on a held feature in the workbook "Depends on" column — which includes every override provider depending on its override host (Product PRD Part 1 §6.3) — is held by the same decision. Decisions that set a parameter or a Proposed design inside an otherwise specified feature do not hold it; they are readiness conditions (table 2).

# 3. Open release decisions


- **P1-SPLIT:** P1 holds 337 of 434 features. Splitting it into P1-core (go-live) and P1-extended is recommended and open.
- **FG-07:** delivery windows (KTDA 8 weeks, Malawi Post 183 days) against build volume — deferred by the user on 2026-11-18.
- **Sizing:** no feature is sized; sizing is done by the Developers. The 308 code-level features are the main sizing load.
- **Before Sprint Planning 1:** P1-SPLIT decided, P1-core ordered by the Product Owner, WF-ENG decided, and the §3.3 holds on the first Sprint's items cleared.
- **Phase does not set order within a phase.** The Product Backlog order is set by the Product Owner and is not yet set (Product PRD Part 1 §11.1).
